======================================================================
ECONOMICS KNOWLEDGE BASE
======================================================================

Entry 1
Category: Microeconomics
Concept: Economics
Definition: The social science that studies how scarce resources are allocated.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in economics.
- Important in business, public policy and finance.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Economics is an important economics concept.
Related Concepts:
Markets, Banking, Finance, GDP, Inflation, Trade

Entry 2
Category: Microeconomics
Concept: Scarcity
Definition: The condition where resources are limited but human wants are unlimited.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in economics.
- Important in business, public policy and finance.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Scarcity is an important economics concept.
Related Concepts:
Markets, Banking, Finance, GDP, Inflation, Trade

Entry 3
Category: Microeconomics
Concept: Opportunity Cost
Definition: The value of the next best alternative forgone.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in economics.
- Important in business, public policy and finance.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Choosing to attend university may mean giving up full-time earnings.
Related Concepts:
Markets, Banking, Finance, GDP, Inflation, Trade

Entry 4
Category: Microeconomics
Concept: Demand

- Important in business, public policy and finance.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Demand is an important economics concept.

Entry 6
Category: Microeconomics
Concept: Market Equilibrium
Definition: The point where demand equals supply.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in economics.
- Important in business, public policy and finance.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
When supply equals demand, shortages and surpluses disappear.
Related Concepts:
Markets, Banking, Finance, GDP, Inflation, Trade
